            <div class="page-padding">\r\n
                <div x-data="{\r\n
        copySuccess: false,\r\n
        copyTable() {\r\n
            let rows = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('table tbody tr')).filter(r => r.querySelectorAll('td').length > 1);\r\n
            if (rows.length === 0) return;\r\n
            let text = 'Date\tCategory\tReference No\tExpense For\tAmount\tPayment Type\tAccount\tNote\n';\r\n
            rows.forEach(r => {\r\n
                let cells = r.querySelectorAll('td');\r\n
                if (cells.length >= 8) {\r\n
                    let date = cells[0].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    let cat = cells[1].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    let ref = cells[2].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    let ef = cells[3].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    let amt = cells[4].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    let pt = cells[5].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    let acc = cells[6].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    let note = cells[7].innerText.trim();\r\n
                    text += `${date}\t${cat}\t${ref}\t${ef}\t${amt}\t${pt}\t${acc}\t${note}\n`;\r\n
                }\r\n
            });\r\n
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {\r\n
                this.copySuccess = true;\r\n
                setTimeout(() => this.copySuccess = false, 2000);\r\n
            });\r\n
        }\r\n
    }" class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">\r\n
\r\n

                <!-- Page Size Selector (Phase 6: wired to per_page) -->\r\n
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">\r\n

    x-data="{ amountTipOpen: false }"\r\n
            @click="amountTipOpen = !amountTipOpen"\r\n

    @keydown.escape.window="amountTipOpen = false"\r\n
    :data-exact-amount="(Number(10) || 0).toFixed(2)"\r\n
    class="relative inline-flex group cursor-help"\r\n
>\r\n
    <span class="tabular-nums" x-text="'$' + window.formatCompactAmount(10).compact"></span>\r\n
\r\n

    </span>\r\n
</span>                                    </span>\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2">\r\n
                                    <span class="px-2 py-0.5 bg-background dark:bg-slate-800 rounded font-black text-[8px] uppercase tracking-widest text-text-muted">\r\n
                                        Cash\r\n
                                    </span>\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-black text-text-muted uppercase tracking-tight">\r\n
                                    ---\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium text-text-muted italic">---</td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-center">\r\n
                                    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        open: false,\n
        triggerRect: null,\n
        panelVisible: false,\n
\n
        toggle() {\n
            if (this.open) {\n
                this.close();\n
            } else {\n
                this.open = true;\n
                this.panelVisible = false;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        captureTrigger() {\n
            const trigger = this.$el.querySelector('[data-dropdown-trigger]');\n
            if (trigger) {\n
                this.triggerRect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        close() {\n
            this.open = false;\n
            this.panelVisible = false;\n
        },\n
\n
        openPanel() {\n
            if (!this.open) return;\n
\n
            this.captureTrigger();\n
\n
            // Stash the bound handler so we can remove it on close.\n
            if (!this._boundReposition) {\n
                this._boundReposition = () => {\n
                    if (this.open) this.captureTrigger();\n
                };\n
                window.addEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.addEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
            }\n
\n
            // If the trigger ever leaves the viewport (scrolled out), close.\n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                const r = this.triggerRect;\n
                if (r && (r.bottom < 0 || r.top > window.innerHeight || r.right < 0 || r.left > window.innerWidth)) {\n
                    this.close();\n
                } else {\n
                    this.panelVisible = true;\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        closePanel() {\n
            if (this._boundReposition) {\n
                window.removeEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.removeEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
                this._boundReposition = null;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        get panelStyle() {\n
            // No rect yet (e.g. during the open tick): park the panel off-screen\n
            // so it never flashes at the viewport origin.\n
            if (!this.triggerRect) {\n
                return 'position: fixed; top: -9999px; left: -9999px; z-index: 60; visibility: hidden;';\n
            }\n
\n
            const r = this.triggerRect;\n
            const gap = 8; // ~mt-2\n
            const style = {\n
                position: 'fixed',\n
                top: (r.bottom + gap) + 'px',\n
                zIndex: 60,\n
                visibility: this.panelVisible ? 'visible' : 'hidden',\n
            };\n
\n
            if (false) {\n
                style.left = r.left + 'px';\n
            } else {\n
                // align right (default): right-align panel to the trigger's right edge.\n
                style.right = (window.innerWidth - r.right) + 'px';\n
            }\n
\n
            return Object.entries(style).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('; ');\n
        },\n
\n
        init() {\n
            this.$watch('open', (val) => {\n
                if (val) {\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                        this.captureTrigger();\n
                        this.openPanel();\n
                    });\n
                } else {\n
                    this.closePanel();\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
    }"\n
    @click.outside="close()"\n
    @close.stop="close()"\n
    class="relative"\n
>\n
    <div class="inline-flex" data-dropdown-trigger @click="toggle()">\n
        <button type="button" class="btn-primary px-3 py-1 !text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1.5 leading-none">\r\n
                                                Action\r\n
                                                <svg class="w-2 h-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2.5" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7"></path></svg>\r\n
                                            </button>\n
    </div>\n
\n
    \n
    <template x-teleport="body">\n
        <div\n
            x-show="open"\n
            x-cloak\n
            x-transition:enter="transition ease-out duration-200"\n
            x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 scale-95"\n
            x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 scale-100"\n
            x-transition:leave="transition ease-in duration-75"\n
            x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 scale-100"\n
            x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 scale-95"\n
            :style="panelStyle"\n
            @click="open = false"\n
            class="fixed rounded-lg shadow-dropdown border border-border dark:border-dark-border bg-card dark:bg-dark-card 40 origin-top-right"\n
        >\n
            <div class="rounded-lg py-1.5 py-1 bg-white">\n
                <a class="block w-full px-4 py-2 text-start text-sm leading-5 text-text-primary dark:text-dark-text hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:bg-slate-100 dark:focus:bg-slate-800 transition duration-150 ease-in-out" href="http://localhost:8000/expenses/edit/12">Edit</a>\n
                                            <form action="http://localhost:8000/expenses/delete/12" method="POST" onsubmit="return confirm('Delete and reverse this expense? Its ledger impact will be reversed with an EXPENSE REVERSAL entry.');">\r\n
                                                <input type="hidden" name="_token" value="eeOuvIXZGDWR9SmSGNBeqQRG1ilmDbOCOznktUhK" autocomplete="off">                                                <input type="hidden" name="_method" value="DELETE">                                                <button type="submit" class="w-full text-left block px-4 py-2 text-sm text-danger hover:bg-danger-light dark:hover:bg-danger/10 transition-colors uppercase tracking-widest">Delete</button>\r\n
                                            </form>\n
            </div>\n
        </div>\n
    </template>\n
</div>\n

    @close.stop="close()"\n
    class="relative"\n
>\n

    x-data="{ amountTipOpen: false }"\r\n
            @click="amountTipOpen = !amountTipOpen"\r\n
        @click.away="amountTipOpen = false"\r\n
    @keydown.escape.window="amountTipOpen = false"\r\n
    :data-exact-amount="(Number(10) || 0).toFixed(2)"\r\n
    class="relative inline-flex group cursor-help"\r\n
>\r\n
    <span class="tabular-nums" x-text="'$' + window.formatCompactAmount(10).compact"></span>\r\n
\r\n
    <span class="amount-tip" :class="{ 'tip-open': amountTipOpen }" x-cloak role="tooltip">\r\n
        <span class="text-slate-400">$</span><span class="font-black" x-text="window.formatCompactAmount(10).exact"></span>\r\n
    </span>\r\n
</span>                                    </span>\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2">\r\n
                                    <span class="px-2 py-0.5 bg-background dark:bg-slate-800 rounded font-black text-[8px] uppercase tracking-widest text-text-muted">\r\n
                                        Cash\r\n
                                    </span>\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-black text-text-muted uppercase tracking-tight">\r\n
                                    ---\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium text-text-muted italic">---</td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-center">\r\n
                                    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        open: false,\n
        triggerRect: null,\n
        panelVisible: false,\n
\n
        toggle() {\n
            if (this.open) {\n
                this.close();\n
            } else {\n
                this.open = true;\n
                this.panelVisible = false;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        captureTrigger() {\n
            const trigger = this.$el.querySelector('[data-dropdown-trigger]');\n
            if (trigger) {\n
                this.triggerRect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        close() {\n
            this.open = false;\n
            this.panelVisible = false;\n
        },\n
\n
        openPanel() {\n
            if (!this.open) return;\n
\n
            this.captureTrigger();\n
\n
            // Stash the bound handler so we can remove it on close.\n
            if (!this._boundReposition) {\n
                this._boundReposition = () => {\n
                    if (this.open) this.captureTrigger();\n
                };\n
                window.addEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.addEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
            }\n
\n
            // If the trigger ever leaves the viewport (scrolled out), close.\n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                const r = this.triggerRect;\n
                if (r && (r.bottom < 0 || r.top > window.innerHeight || r.right < 0 || r.left > window.innerWidth)) {\n
                    this.close();\n
                } else {\n
                    this.panelVisible = true;\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        closePanel() {\n
            if (this._boundReposition) {\n
                window.removeEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.removeEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
                this._boundReposition = null;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        get panelStyle() {\n
            // No rect yet (e.g. during the open tick): park the panel off-screen\n
            // so it never flashes at the viewport origin.\n
            if (!this.triggerRect) {\n
                return 'position: fixed; top: -9999px; left: -9999px; z-index: 60; visibility: hidden;';\n
            }\n
\n
            const r = this.triggerRect;\n
            const gap = 8; // ~mt-2\n
            const style = {\n
                position: 'fixed',\n
                top: (r.bottom + gap) + 'px',\n
                zIndex: 60,\n
                visibility: this.panelVisible ? 'visible' : 'hidden',\n
            };\n
\n
            if (false) {\n
                style.left = r.left + 'px';\n
            } else {\n
                // align right (default): right-align panel to the trigger's right edge.\n
                style.right = (window.innerWidth - r.right) + 'px';\n
            }\n
\n
            return Object.entries(style).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('; ');\n
        },\n
\n
        init() {\n
            this.$watch('open', (val) => {\n
                if (val) {\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                        this.captureTrigger();\n
                        this.openPanel();\n
                    });\n
                } else {\n
                    this.closePanel();\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
    }"\n
    @click.outside="close()"\n
    @close.stop="close()"\n

                    this.closePanel();\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
    }"\n
    @click.outside="close()"\n
    @close.stop="close()"\n
    class="relative"\n

    @keydown.escape.window="amountTipOpen = false"\r\n

>\r\n

\r\n

    </span>\r\n
</span>                                    </span>\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2">\r\n

                                        Cash\r\n
                                    </span>\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-black text-text-muted uppercase tracking-tight">\r\n
                                    ---\r\n
                                </td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-medium text-text-muted italic">---</td>\r\n
                                <td class="px-4 py-2 text-center">\r\n
                                    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        open: false,\n
        triggerRect: null,\n
        panelVisible: false,\n
\n
        toggle() {\n
            if (this.open) {\n
                this.close();\n
            } else {\n
                this.open = true;\n
                this.panelVisible = false;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        captureTrigger() {\n
            const trigger = this.$el.querySelector('[data-dropdown-trigger]');\n
            if (trigger) {\n
                this.triggerRect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        close() {\n
            this.open = false;\n
            this.panelVisible = false;\n
        },\n
\n
        openPanel() {\n
            if (!this.open) return;\n
\n
            this.captureTrigger();\n
\n
            // Stash the bound handler so we can remove it on close.\n
            if (!this._boundReposition) {\n
                this._boundReposition = () => {\n
                    if (this.open) this.captureTrigger();\n
                };\n
                window.addEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.addEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
            }\n
\n
            // If the trigger ever leaves the viewport (scrolled out), close.\n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                const r = this.triggerRect;\n
                if (r && (r.bottom < 0 || r.top > window.innerHeight || r.right < 0 || r.left > window.innerWidth)) {\n
                    this.close();\n
                } else {\n
                    this.panelVisible = true;\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        closePanel() {\n
            if (this._boundReposition) {\n
                window.removeEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.removeEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
                this._boundReposition = null;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        get panelStyle() {\n
            // No rect yet (e.g. during the open tick): park the panel off-screen\n
            // so it never flashes at the viewport origin.\n
            if (!this.triggerRect) {\n
                return 'position: fixed; top: -9999px; left: -9999px; z-index: 60; visibility: hidden;';\n
            }\n
\n
            const r = this.triggerRect;\n
            const gap = 8; // ~mt-2\n
            const style = {\n
                position: 'fixed',\n
                top: (r.bottom + gap) + 'px',\n
                zIndex: 60,\n
                visibility: this.panelVisible ? 'visible' : 'hidden',\n
            };\n
\n
            if (false) {\n
                style.left = r.left + 'px';\n
            } else {\n
                // align right (default): right-align panel to the trigger's right edge.\n
                style.right = (window.innerWidth - r.right) + 'px';\n
            }\n
\n
            return Object.entries(style).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('; ');\n
        },\n
\n
        init() {\n
            this.$watch('open', (val) => {\n
                if (val) {\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                        this.captureTrigger();\n
                        this.openPanel();\n
                    });\n
                } else {\n
                    this.closePanel();\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
    }"\n
    @click.outside="close()"\n
    @close.stop="close()"\n
    class="relative"\n
>\n

                                    <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        open: false,\n
        triggerRect: null,\n
        panelVisible: false,\n
\n
        toggle() {\n
            if (this.open) {\n
                this.close();\n
            } else {\n
                this.open = true;\n
                this.panelVisible = false;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        captureTrigger() {\n
            const trigger = this.$el.querySelector('[data-dropdown-trigger]');\n
            if (trigger) {\n
                this.triggerRect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        close() {\n
            this.open = false;\n
            this.panelVisible = false;\n
        },\n
\n
        openPanel() {\n
            if (!this.open) return;\n
\n
            this.captureTrigger();\n
\n
            // Stash the bound handler so we can remove it on close.\n
            if (!this._boundReposition) {\n
                this._boundReposition = () => {\n
                    if (this.open) this.captureTrigger();\n
                };\n
                window.addEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.addEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
            }\n
\n
            // If the trigger ever leaves the viewport (scrolled out), close.\n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                const r = this.triggerRect;\n
                if (r && (r.bottom < 0 || r.top > window.innerHeight || r.right < 0 || r.left > window.innerWidth)) {\n
                    this.close();\n
                } else {\n
                    this.panelVisible = true;\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        closePanel() {\n
            if (this._boundReposition) {\n
                window.removeEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.removeEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
                this._boundReposition = null;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        get panelStyle() {\n
            // No rect yet (e.g. during the open tick): park the panel off-screen\n
            // so it never flashes at the viewport origin.\n
            if (!this.triggerRect) {\n
                return 'position: fixed; top: -9999px; left: -9999px; z-index: 60; visibility: hidden;';\n
            }\n
\n
            const r = this.triggerRect;\n
            const gap = 8; // ~mt-2\n
            const style = {\n
                position: 'fixed',\n
                top: (r.bottom + gap) + 'px',\n
                zIndex: 60,\n
                visibility: this.panelVisible ? 'visible' : 'hidden',\n
            };\n
\n
            if (false) {\n
                style.left = r.left + 'px';\n
            } else {\n
                // align right (default): right-align panel to the trigger's right edge.\n
                style.right = (window.innerWidth - r.right) + 'px';\n
            }\n
\n
            return Object.entries(style).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('; ');\n
        },\n
\n
        init() {\n
            this.$watch('open', (val) => {\n
                if (val) {\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                        this.captureTrigger();\n
                        this.openPanel();\n
                    });\n
                } else {\n
                    this.closePanel();\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
    }"\n

    <div\r\n
        x-data="{\r\n
            open: false,\r\n
            query: '',\r\n
            results: {},\r\n
            totalCount: 0,\r\n
            loading: false,\r\n
            activeIdx: -1,\r\n
            searchRequestId: 0,\r\n
            searchController: null,\r\n
\r\n
            icons: {\r\n
                'package':            'M21 16V8a2 2 0 00-1-1.73l-7-4a2 2 0 00-2 0l-7 4A2 2 0 003 8v8a2 2 0 001 1.73l7 4a2 2 0 002 0l7-4A2 2 0 0021 16z',\r\n
                'wrench':             'M14.7 6.3a1 1 0 000 1.4l1.6 1.6a1 1 0 001.4 0l3.77-3.77a6 6 0 01-7.94 7.94l-6.91 6.91a2.12 2.12 0 01-3-3l6.91-6.91a6 6 0 017.94-7.94l-3.76 3.76z',\r\n
                'user':               'M20 21v-2a4 4 0 00-4-4H8a4 4 0 00-4 4v2M12 11a4 4 0 100-8 4 4 0 000 8z',\r\n
                'truck':              'M5 17H3a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h11a2 2 0 012 2v3m0 0h4l3 3v4h-7V8zM16 17a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0zM7 17a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0z',\r\n
                'file-text':          'M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8zM14 2v6h6M16 13H8M16 17H8M10 9H8',\r\n
                'shopping-cart':      'M6 2L3 6v14a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V6l-3-4zM3 6h18M16 10a4 4 0 01-8 0',\r\n
                'clipboard-list':     'M9 5H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2h10a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2h-2M9 5a2 2 0 000 4h6a2 2 0 000-4M9 5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2M9 12h6M9 16h4',\r\n
                'rotate-ccw':         'M1 4v6h6M3.51 15a9 9 0 101.85-4.36L1 10',\r\n
                'undo-2':             'M9 14L4 9l5-5M4 9h10.5a5.5 5.5 0 010 11H11',\r\n
                'arrow-left-right':   'M17 8l4 4-4 4M7 16l-4-4 4-4M3 12h18',\r\n
                'sliders-horizontal': 'M21 4H8M3 4h1M17 9h1M3 9h10M21 14H11M3 14h4M7 20h1M3 20h0',\r\n
                'receipt':            'M9 5H7a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2h10a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2h-2M9 5a2 2 0 000 4h6a2 2 0 000-4M9 5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2',\r\n
                'shield-check':       'M12 22s8-4 8-10V5l-8-3-8 3v7c0 6 8 10 8 10zM9 12l2 2 4-4',\r\n
                'compass':            'M12 2a10 10 0 100 20 10 10 0 000-20zm3.59 7.41l-2.18 5.82a1 1 0 01-.58.58l-5.82 2.18a.5.5 0 01-.64-.64l2.18-5.82a1 1 0 01.58-.58l5.82-2.18a.5.5 0 01.64.64z',\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            openModal() {\r\n
                this.open = true;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                this.$nextTick(() => {\r\n
                    const el = document.getElementById('global-command-palette-input');\r\n
                    if (el) el.focus();\r\n
                });\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            closeModal() {\r\n
                this.open = false;\r\n
                this.query = '';\r\n
                this.results = {};\r\n
                this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            get flatResults() {\r\n
                const flat = [];\r\n
                for (const key in this.results) {\r\n
                    if (this.results[key] && this.results[key].items) {\r\n
                        this.results[key].items.forEach(item => flat.push(item));\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
                return flat;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            async doSearch() {\r\n
                const q = this.query.trim();\r\n
                const requestId = ++this.searchRequestId;\r\n
                if (this.searchController) {\r\n
                    this.searchController.abort();\r\n
                }\r\n
                if (q.length < 2) {\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                    this.loading = false;\r\n
                    return;\r\n
                }\r\n
                this.loading = true;\r\n
                const controller = new AbortController();\r\n
                this.searchController = controller;\r\n
                try {\r\n
                    const res = await fetch('http://localhost:8000/global-search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q), {\r\n
                        signal: controller.signal,\r\n
                        headers: {\r\n
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name=csrf-token]').content,\r\n
                            'Accept': 'application/json',\r\n
                        },\r\n
                    });\r\n
                    const data = await res.json();\r\n
                    if (requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = data.categories || {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = data.total_count || 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                } catch (e) {\r\n
                    if (e.name === 'AbortError' || requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                } finally {\r\n
                    if (requestId === this.searchRequestId) {\r\n
                        this.loading = false;\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            navigate(dir) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (!flat.length) return;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(flat.length - 1, this.activeIdx + dir));\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            selectActive() {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx]) {\r\n
                    window.location.href = flat[this.activeIdx].url;\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            isActive(item) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                return this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx] && flat[this.activeIdx].url === item.url;\r\n
            },\r\n
        }"\r\n
        @open-global-search.window="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.cmd.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.ctrl.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.escape.window="closeModal()"\r\n
        x-show="open"\r\n
        x-cloak\r\n
        class="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto"\r\n
        role="dialog"\r\n
        aria-modal="true">\r\n
\r\n
        \r\n
        <div\r\n
            x-show="open"\r\n
            x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-200"\r\n
            x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"\r\n
            x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"\r\n
            x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-150"\r\n
            x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"\r\n
            x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0"\r\n
            @click="closeModal()"\r\n
            class="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity"></div>\r\n
\r\n

                        </svg>\r\n

<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
        Alpine.store('notify', {\n
            toasts: [],\n
            _idCounter: 0,\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show a success toast.\n
             * @param {string} message\n
             * @param {number} duration  Auto-dismiss ms. Default 4000. 0 = never.\n
             */\n
            showSuccess(message, duration = 4000) {\n
                this._add('success', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show an error toast.\n
             * @param {string|string[]} message  Single string or array of validation errors.\n
             * @param {number}          duration  Default 0 = stays until dismissed.\n
             */\n
            showError(message, duration = 0) {\n
                // Normalize: if Laravel validation errors object {field: [msgs]} convert to flat array\n
                if (message && typeof message === 'object' && !Array.isArray(message)) {\n
                    message = Object.values(message).flat();\n
                }\n
                this._add('error', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * @private\n
             */\n
            _add(type, messages, duration) {\n
                const id = ++this._idCounter;\n
                const toast = { id, type, messages, duration, visible: true, progress: 100 };\n
                this.toasts.push(toast);\n
\n
                if (duration > 0) {\n
                    // After a short delay, start the progress bar shrink\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        const t = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                        if (t) t.progress = 0;\n
                    }, 50);\n
\n
                    // Auto-dismiss\n
                    setTimeout(() => this.dismiss(id), duration);\n
                }\n
\n
                // Keep max 5 toasts visible at once — remove oldest\n
                if (this.toasts.length > 5) {\n
                    this.dismiss(this.toasts[0].id);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            dismiss(id) {\n
                const toast = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                if (toast) {\n
                    toast.visible = false;\n
                    // Remove from array after leave animation completes\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        this.toasts = this.toasts.filter(t => t.id !== id);\n
                    }, 300);\n
                }\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        queueMicrotask(() => {\n
                                            });\n
    });\n
\n
    /**\n
     * ── Global convenience aliases ─────────────────────────────────\n
     * Callable from ANY script tag, AJAX handler, or Alpine component:\n
     *\n
     *   showSuccess('Item saved successfully.');\n
     *   showError('The brand name has already been taken.');\n
     *   showError(['Name required.', 'Price must be numeric.']);\n
     */\n
    window.showSuccess = (msg, duration = 4000) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    window.showError = (msg, duration = 0) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
</script>\n\r
\n
    \r\n
    \r\n
    \r\n
    <div x-data="keyboardShortcutsHandler()" \n
     x-init="initShortcuts()"\n
     @open-shortcuts-help.window="openHelpModal()"\n
     class="relative z-50">\n
\n
    \n
    <div x-show="inSequence"\n
         x-transition:enter="transition ease-out duration-200"\n
         x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n
         x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"\n
         x-transition:leave="transition ease-in duration-150"\n
         x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"\n
         x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n
         class="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 bg-slate-900/95 dark:bg-slate-800/95 text-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700/80 backdrop-blur-md"\n
         x-cloak>\n

                <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Shortcut Sequence:</span>\n

                <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-mono font-bold bg-primary-500/20 text-primary-300 rounded border border-primary-400/30 animate-pulse">_</span>\n

    function keyboardShortcutsHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            inSequence: false,\n
            pendingModuleKey: null,\n
            activeModuleName: '',\n
            sequenceTimer: null,\n
            timeoutDuration: 1800, // ~1.8 seconds timeout\n
\n
            initShortcuts() {\n
                window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleKeyDown(e));\n
            },\n
\n
            isEditableTarget(target) {\n
                if (!target) return false;\n
                const tagName = target.tagName ? target.tagName.toUpperCase() : '';\n
                if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(tagName)) return true;\n
                if (target.isContentEditable) return true;\n
                if (target.closest && target.closest('[data-global-search-modal]')) {\n
                    return true;\n
                }\n
                return false;\n
            },\n
\n
            openHelpModal() {\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('toggle-shortcuts-modal', { detail: { open: true } }));\n
            },\n
\n
            handleKeyDown(e) {\n
                // Disabled on mobile and tablet screens (< 1024px)\n
                if (window.innerWidth < 1024) return;\n
\n
                // Never intercept if focused on input, textarea, select, or search box\n
                if (this.isEditableTarget(e.target)) return;\n
\n
                const key = e.key ? e.key.toUpperCase() : '';\n
\n
                // If in sequence mode\n
                if (this.inSequence) {\n
                    if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.cancelSequence();\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Ignore standalone modifier presses while waiting for the 2nd key\n
                    if (['CONTROL', 'ALT', 'SHIFT', 'META'].includes(key)) {\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.executeSecondKey(key);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Check for Help Modal shortcut: Ctrl+Alt+? or Shift+?\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && (e.key === '?' || e.key === '/')) {\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.openHelpModal();\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Trigger First Key: Ctrl + Alt + [Module Key]\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && !e.shiftKey && !e.metaKey) {\n
                    if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS && window.APP_SHORTCUTS[key]) {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.startSequence(key);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            startSequence(moduleKey) {\n
                this.inSequence = true;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = moduleKey;\n
                this.activeModuleName = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[moduleKey]?.module || moduleKey;\n
\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                this.sequenceTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n
                    this.cancelSequence();\n
                }, this.timeoutDuration);\n
            },\n
\n
            cancelSequence() {\n
                this.inSequence = false;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = null;\n
                this.activeModuleName = '';\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) {\n
                    clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                    this.sequenceTimer = null;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            executeSecondKey(pageKey) {\n
                const moduleData = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[this.pendingModuleKey];\n
                this.cancelSequence();\n
\n
                if (!moduleData || !moduleData.items) return;\n
\n
                const targetItem = moduleData.items.find(item => item.page_key === pageKey);\n
\n
                if (!targetItem) {\n
                    // Second key not found in this module\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Strict permission enforcement\n
                if (!targetItem.has_permission) {\n
                    if (typeof window.showError === 'function') {\n
                        window.showError("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    } else {\n
                        alert("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    }\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Navigate to target route\n
                if (targetItem.url && targetItem.url !== '#') {\n
                    window.location.href = targetItem.url;\n
                }\n
            }\n
        };\n
    }\n
</script>\n
    <div x-data="shortcutsModalHandler()"\n
     @toggle-shortcuts-modal.window="openModal($event.detail?.open ?? true)"\n
     @keydown.escape.window="closeModal()"\n
     class="relative z-50">\n
\n
    \n

<script>\n
    function shortcutsModalHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            isOpen: false,\n
            searchQuery: '',\n
            expandedModules: {},\n
            allExpanded: true,\n
\n
            init() {\n
                // By default expand all modules\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = true;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            openModal(open = true) {\n
                this.isOpen = open;\n
                if (open) {\n
                    this.init();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            closeModal() {\n
                this.isOpen = false;\n
                this.searchQuery = '';\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleModule(key) {\n
                this.expandedModules[key] = !this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            isModuleExpanded(key) {\n
                if (this.searchQuery.trim().length > 0) return true;\n
                return !!this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleAll() {\n
                this.allExpanded = !this.allExpanded;\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = this.allExpanded;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            get filteredModules() {\n
                if (!window.APP_SHORTCUTS) return {};\n
                const q = this.searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\n
                if (!q) return window.APP_SHORTCUTS;\n
\n
                const result = {};\n
                Object.entries(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(([modKey, mod]) => {\n
                    const modMatches = mod.module.toLowerCase().includes(q) || ('ctrl+alt+' + modKey.toLowerCase()).includes(q);\n
                    const matchingItems = mod.items.filter(item => {\n
                        return modMatches ||\n
                               item.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n
                               item.page_key.toLowerCase() === q ||\n
                               item.sequence.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n
                    });\n
\n
                    if (matchingItems.length > 0) {\n
                        result[modKey] = {\n
                            ...mod,\n
                            items: matchingItems\n
                        };\n
                    }\n
                });\n
                return result;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    }\n
</script>\n\r

                'file-text':          'M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8zM14 2v6h6M16 13H8M16 17H8M10 9H8',\r\n

            },\r\n
\r\n
            openModal() {\r\n
                this.open = true;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                this.$nextTick(() => {\r\n
                    const el = document.getElementById('global-command-palette-input');\r\n
                    if (el) el.focus();\r\n
                });\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            closeModal() {\r\n
                this.open = false;\r\n
                this.query = '';\r\n
                this.results = {};\r\n
                this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            get flatResults() {\r\n
                const flat = [];\r\n
                for (const key in this.results) {\r\n
                    if (this.results[key] && this.results[key].items) {\r\n
                        this.results[key].items.forEach(item => flat.push(item));\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
                return flat;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            async doSearch() {\r\n
                const q = this.query.trim();\r\n
                const requestId = ++this.searchRequestId;\r\n
                if (this.searchController) {\r\n
                    this.searchController.abort();\r\n
                }\r\n
                if (q.length < 2) {\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                    this.loading = false;\r\n
                    return;\r\n
                }\r\n
                this.loading = true;\r\n
                const controller = new AbortController();\r\n
                this.searchController = controller;\r\n
                try {\r\n
                    const res = await fetch('http://localhost:8000/global-search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q), {\r\n
                        signal: controller.signal,\r\n
                        headers: {\r\n
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name=csrf-token]').content,\r\n
                            'Accept': 'application/json',\r\n
                        },\r\n
                    });\r\n
                    const data = await res.json();\r\n
                    if (requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = data.categories || {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = data.total_count || 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                } catch (e) {\r\n
                    if (e.name === 'AbortError' || requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                } finally {\r\n
                    if (requestId === this.searchRequestId) {\r\n
                        this.loading = false;\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            navigate(dir) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (!flat.length) return;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(flat.length - 1, this.activeIdx + dir));\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            selectActive() {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx]) {\r\n
                    window.location.href = flat[this.activeIdx].url;\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            isActive(item) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                return this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx] && flat[this.activeIdx].url === item.url;\r\n
            },\r\n
        }"\r\n
        @open-global-search.window="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.cmd.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.ctrl.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.escape.window="closeModal()"\r\n

<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
        Alpine.store('notify', {\n
            toasts: [],\n
            _idCounter: 0,\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show a success toast.\n
             * @param {string} message\n
             * @param {number} duration  Auto-dismiss ms. Default 4000. 0 = never.\n
             */\n
            showSuccess(message, duration = 4000) {\n
                this._add('success', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show an error toast.\n
             * @param {string|string[]} message  Single string or array of validation errors.\n
             * @param {number}          duration  Default 0 = stays until dismissed.\n
             */\n
            showError(message, duration = 0) {\n
                // Normalize: if Laravel validation errors object {field: [msgs]} convert to flat array\n
                if (message && typeof message === 'object' && !Array.isArray(message)) {\n
                    message = Object.values(message).flat();\n
                }\n
                this._add('error', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * @private\n
             */\n
            _add(type, messages, duration) {\n
                const id = ++this._idCounter;\n
                const toast = { id, type, messages, duration, visible: true, progress: 100 };\n
                this.toasts.push(toast);\n
\n
                if (duration > 0) {\n
                    // After a short delay, start the progress bar shrink\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        const t = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                        if (t) t.progress = 0;\n
                    }, 50);\n
\n
                    // Auto-dismiss\n
                    setTimeout(() => this.dismiss(id), duration);\n
                }\n
\n
                // Keep max 5 toasts visible at once — remove oldest\n
                if (this.toasts.length > 5) {\n
                    this.dismiss(this.toasts[0].id);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            dismiss(id) {\n
                const toast = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                if (toast) {\n
                    toast.visible = false;\n
                    // Remove from array after leave animation completes\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        this.toasts = this.toasts.filter(t => t.id !== id);\n
                    }, 300);\n
                }\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        queueMicrotask(() => {\n
                                            });\n
    });\n
\n
    /**\n
     * ── Global convenience aliases ─────────────────────────────────\n
     * Callable from ANY script tag, AJAX handler, or Alpine component:\n
     *\n
     *   showSuccess('Item saved successfully.');\n
     *   showError('The brand name has already been taken.');\n
     *   showError(['Name required.', 'Price must be numeric.']);\n
     */\n
    window.showSuccess = (msg, duration = 4000) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    window.showError = (msg, duration = 0) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
</script>\n\r
\n
    \r\n
    \r\n
    \r\n

     x-init="initShortcuts()"\n
     @open-shortcuts-help.window="openHelpModal()"\n
     class="relative z-50">\n
\n
    \n
    <div x-show="inSequence"\n
         x-transition:enter="transition ease-out duration-200"\n
         x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n
         x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"\n
         x-transition:leave="transition ease-in duration-150"\n
         x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"\n
         x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n
         class="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 bg-slate-900/95 dark:bg-slate-800/95 text-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700/80 backdrop-blur-md"\n
         x-cloak>\n
        <div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-400 animate-ping"></div>\n
        <div class="flex flex-col">\n
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">\n
                <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Shortcut Sequence:</span>\n
                <span class="text-xs font-black text-primary-400 tracking-wide" x-text="activeModuleName"></span>\n

            </div>\n
            <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Press second key or <kbd class="font-mono bg-slate-800 px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] text-slate-300">Esc</kbd> to cancel</p>\n

    function keyboardShortcutsHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            inSequence: false,\n
            pendingModuleKey: null,\n
            activeModuleName: '',\n
            sequenceTimer: null,\n
            timeoutDuration: 1800, // ~1.8 seconds timeout\n
\n
            initShortcuts() {\n
                window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleKeyDown(e));\n
            },\n
\n
            isEditableTarget(target) {\n
                if (!target) return false;\n
                const tagName = target.tagName ? target.tagName.toUpperCase() : '';\n
                if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(tagName)) return true;\n
                if (target.isContentEditable) return true;\n
                if (target.closest && target.closest('[data-global-search-modal]')) {\n
                    return true;\n
                }\n
                return false;\n
            },\n
\n
            openHelpModal() {\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('toggle-shortcuts-modal', { detail: { open: true } }));\n
            },\n
\n
            handleKeyDown(e) {\n
                // Disabled on mobile and tablet screens (< 1024px)\n
                if (window.innerWidth < 1024) return;\n
\n
                // Never intercept if focused on input, textarea, select, or search box\n
                if (this.isEditableTarget(e.target)) return;\n
\n
                const key = e.key ? e.key.toUpperCase() : '';\n
\n
                // If in sequence mode\n
                if (this.inSequence) {\n
                    if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.cancelSequence();\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Ignore standalone modifier presses while waiting for the 2nd key\n
                    if (['CONTROL', 'ALT', 'SHIFT', 'META'].includes(key)) {\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.executeSecondKey(key);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Check for Help Modal shortcut: Ctrl+Alt+? or Shift+?\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && (e.key === '?' || e.key === '/')) {\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.openHelpModal();\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Trigger First Key: Ctrl + Alt + [Module Key]\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && !e.shiftKey && !e.metaKey) {\n
                    if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS && window.APP_SHORTCUTS[key]) {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.startSequence(key);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            startSequence(moduleKey) {\n
                this.inSequence = true;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = moduleKey;\n
                this.activeModuleName = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[moduleKey]?.module || moduleKey;\n
\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                this.sequenceTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n
                    this.cancelSequence();\n
                }, this.timeoutDuration);\n
            },\n
\n
            cancelSequence() {\n
                this.inSequence = false;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = null;\n
                this.activeModuleName = '';\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) {\n
                    clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                    this.sequenceTimer = null;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            executeSecondKey(pageKey) {\n
                const moduleData = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[this.pendingModuleKey];\n
                this.cancelSequence();\n
\n
                if (!moduleData || !moduleData.items) return;\n
\n
                const targetItem = moduleData.items.find(item => item.page_key === pageKey);\n
\n
                if (!targetItem) {\n
                    // Second key not found in this module\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Strict permission enforcement\n
                if (!targetItem.has_permission) {\n
                    if (typeof window.showError === 'function') {\n
                        window.showError("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    } else {\n
                        alert("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    }\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Navigate to target route\n
                if (targetItem.url && targetItem.url !== '#') {\n
                    window.location.href = targetItem.url;\n
                }\n
            }\n
        };\n
    }\n
</script>\n

<script>\n
    function shortcutsModalHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            isOpen: false,\n
            searchQuery: '',\n
            expandedModules: {},\n
            allExpanded: true,\n
\n
            init() {\n
                // By default expand all modules\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = true;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            openModal(open = true) {\n
                this.isOpen = open;\n
                if (open) {\n
                    this.init();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            closeModal() {\n
                this.isOpen = false;\n
                this.searchQuery = '';\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleModule(key) {\n
                this.expandedModules[key] = !this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            isModuleExpanded(key) {\n
                if (this.searchQuery.trim().length > 0) return true;\n
                return !!this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleAll() {\n
                this.allExpanded = !this.allExpanded;\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = this.allExpanded;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            get filteredModules() {\n
                if (!window.APP_SHORTCUTS) return {};\n
                const q = this.searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\n
                if (!q) return window.APP_SHORTCUTS;\n
\n
                const result = {};\n
                Object.entries(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(([modKey, mod]) => {\n
                    const modMatches = mod.module.toLowerCase().includes(q) || ('ctrl+alt+' + modKey.toLowerCase()).includes(q);\n
                    const matchingItems = mod.items.filter(item => {\n
                        return modMatches ||\n
                               item.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n
                               item.page_key.toLowerCase() === q ||\n
                               item.sequence.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n
                    });\n
\n
                    if (matchingItems.length > 0) {\n
                        result[modKey] = {\n
                            ...mod,\n
                            items: matchingItems\n
                        };\n
                    }\n
                });\n
                return result;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    }\n
</script>\n\r

        options: [{&quot;id&quot;:&quot;1&quot;,&quot;label&quot;:&quot;Credit WH&quot;,&quot;subtext&quot;:null}],\n
        selectedValue: &quot;&quot;,\n
        search: '',\n
        highlightedIndex: -1,\n
        placeholder: &quot;All Warehouses&quot;,\n
        emptyOption: &quot;All Warehouses&quot;,\n
        emptyValue: &quot;&quot;,\n
        init() {\n
            \n
            \n
            this.syncSearchFromSelected();\n
        },\n
        syncSearchFromSelected() {\n
            if (this.selectedValue !== null && this.selectedValue !== '' && this.selectedValue !== this.emptyValue) {\n
                const found = this.options.find(o => String(o.id) === String(this.selectedValue));\n
                if (found) {\n
                    this.search = found.label;\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
            }\n
            if (this.emptyOption && (this.selectedValue === this.emptyValue || this.selectedValue === '' || this.selectedValue === null)) {\n
                this.search = this.emptyOption;\n
            } else {\n
                this.search = '';\n
            }\n
        },\n
        filteredOptions() {\n
            if (!this.search || this.search === this.emptyOption) {\n
                return this.options;\n
            }\n
            const query = this.search.toLowerCase().trim();\n
            const currentSelected = this.options.find(o => String(o.id) === String(this.selectedValue));\n
            if (currentSelected && this.search === currentSelected.label) {\n
                return this.options;\n
            }\n
            return this.options.filter(o => {\n
                const matchLabel = o.label && o.label.toLowerCase().includes(query);\n
                const matchSub = o.subtext && o.subtext.toLowerCase().includes(query);\n
                return matchLabel || matchSub;\n
            });\n
        },\n
        select(option) {\n
            if (option) {\n
                this.selectedValue = String(option.id);\n
                this.search = option.label;\n
            } else {\n
                this.selectedValue = this.emptyValue;\n
                this.search = this.emptyOption || '';\n
            }\n
            this.dropdownOpen = false;\n
            this.highlightedIndex = -1;\n
\n
            \n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                if (this.$refs.hiddenInput) {\n
                    this.$refs.hiddenInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));\n
                    this.$refs.hiddenInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));\n
                }\n
                this.$dispatch('select', { value: this.selectedValue, option: option });\n
                this.$dispatch('input', this.selectedValue);\n
                this.$dispatch('change', this.selectedValue);\n
\n
                                    document.getElementById('filterForm')?.submit();\n
                            });\n
        },\n
        handleInputClick() {\n
            if (this.emptyOption && this.search === this.emptyOption) {\n
                this.search = '';\n
            } else {\n
                const currentSelected = this.options.find(o => String(o.id) === String(this.selectedValue));\n
                if (currentSelected && this.search === currentSelected.label) {\n
                    // select all text for fast replacement on typing\n
                    if (this.$refs.textInput) this.$refs.textInput.select();\n
                }\n
            }\n
            this.dropdownOpen = true;\n
            this.highlightedIndex = -1;\n
        },\n
        handleArrowDown() {\n
            if (!this.dropdownOpen) {\n
                this.dropdownOpen = true;\n
            }\n
            const filtered = this.filteredOptions();\n
            if (filtered.length === 0) return;\n
            this.highlightedIndex = (this.highlightedIndex + 1) % filtered.length;\n
        },\n
        handleArrowUp() {\n
            if (!this.dropdownOpen) {\n
                this.dropdownOpen = true;\n
            }\n
            const filtered = this.filteredOptions();\n
            if (filtered.length === 0) return;\n
            this.highlightedIndex = this.highlightedIndex <= 0 ? filtered.length - 1 : this.highlightedIndex - 1;\n
        },\n
        handleClickAway() {\n
            this.dropdownOpen = false;\n
            this.syncSearchFromSelected();\n
        },\n
        handleEnter() {\n
            if (!this.dropdownOpen) return;\n
            const filtered = this.filteredOptions();\n
            if (filtered.length > 0) {\n
                this.select(filtered[this.highlightedIndex >= 0 ? this.highlightedIndex : 0]);\n
            } else if (this.emptyOption && this.search.toLowerCase().includes(this.emptyOption.toLowerCase())) {\n
                this.select(null);\n
            }\n
        }\n
     }"\n
     @option-added.window="if($event.detail.name === 'warehouse_id' || $event.detail.target === 'warehouse_id' || $event.detail.model === '') {\n
        const newOpt = {\n
            id: String($event.detail.id ?? $event.detail.value),\n
            label: String($event.detail.label ?? $event.detail.name),\n
            subtext: $event.detail.subtext ? String($event.detail.subtext) : null\n
        };\n
        if (!options.some(o => String(o.id) === String(newOpt.id))) {\n
            options.push(newOpt);\n
        }\n
        select(newOpt);\n
     }">\n
\n
    <div class="relative flex-1">\n
                    <input type="hidden"\n
                   x-ref="hiddenInput"\n
                   name="warehouse_id"\n
                                      :value="selectedValue"\n
                   >\n
        \n
        <input type="text"\n
               x-ref="textInput"\n
               x-model="search"\n
               @click="handleInputClick()"\n
               @input="dropdownOpen = true; highlightedIndex = -1"\n
               @click.away="handleClickAway()"\n
               @keydown.arrowdown.prevent="handleArrowDown()"\n
               @keydown.arrowup.prevent="handleArrowUp()"\n
               @keydown.enter.prevent="handleEnter()"\n
               @keydown.escape="handleClickAway()"\n
               placeholder="All Warehouses"\n
                              class="w-full bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border border-slate-100 dark:border-dark-border rounded-xl py-2 px-4 pr-9 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 placeholder-slate-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-primary-500 transition-all outline-none ">\n
\n
        <!-- Chevron Icon -->\n
        <div class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none text-slate-400">\n
            <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-150" :class="dropdownOpen ? 'rotate-180 text-primary-500' : ''" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">\n
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2.5" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7"></path>\n
            </svg>\n
        </div>\n
\n
        <!-- Dropdown Menu -->\n
        <div x-show="dropdownOpen"\n
             x-cloak\n
             x-transition:enter="transition ease-out duration-100"\n
             x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 scale-95"\n
             x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 scale-100"\n
             class="absolute z-50 w-full mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-dark-card border border-slate-100 dark:border-dark-border rounded-xl shadow-xl max-h-40 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">\n
            <div class="p-1">\n
                                    <div @click="select(null)"\n
                         class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 cursor-pointer rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 italic transition-colors border-b border-slate-50 dark:border-dark-border mb-1"\n
                         :class="(selectedValue === emptyValue || !selectedValue) ? 'bg-primary-50/50 dark:bg-primary-500/10 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400' : ''">\n
                        All Warehouses\n
                    </div>\n
                \n
                <template x-for="option in filteredOptions()" :key="option.id">\n
                    <div @click="select(option)"\n
                         class="px-4 py-2 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 cursor-pointer rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold transition-colors"\n
                        :class="String(selectedValue) === String(option.id) ? 'bg-primary-50 dark:bg-primary-500/20 text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400' : (filteredOptions().indexOf(option) === highlightedIndex ? 'bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300')">\n
                        <div class="flex flex-col">\n
                            <span x-text="option.label" class="truncate"></span>\n
                            <span x-show="option.subtext" class="text-[9px] text-slate-400" x-text="option.subtext"></span>\n
                        </div>\n
                    </div>\n
                </template>\n
\n
                <div x-show="filteredOptions().length === 0" class="px-4 py-2 text-[10px] text-slate-400 italic text-center">\n
                    No results found\n
                </div>\n
            </div>\n
        </div>\n
    </div>\n
\n
    </div>\n
                    </div>\r\n

                                    <td class="px-4 py-2 text-center">\r\n
                                        <div\n
    x-data="{\n
        open: false,\n
        triggerRect: null,\n
        panelVisible: false,\n
\n
        toggle() {\n
            if (this.open) {\n
                this.close();\n
            } else {\n
                this.open = true;\n
                this.panelVisible = false;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        captureTrigger() {\n
            const trigger = this.$el.querySelector('[data-dropdown-trigger]');\n
            if (trigger) {\n
                this.triggerRect = trigger.getBoundingClientRect();\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        close() {\n
            this.open = false;\n
            this.panelVisible = false;\n
        },\n
\n
        openPanel() {\n
            if (!this.open) return;\n
\n
            this.captureTrigger();\n
\n
            // Stash the bound handler so we can remove it on close.\n
            if (!this._boundReposition) {\n
                this._boundReposition = () => {\n
                    if (this.open) this.captureTrigger();\n
                };\n
                window.addEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.addEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
            }\n
\n
            // If the trigger ever leaves the viewport (scrolled out), close.\n
            this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                const r = this.triggerRect;\n
                if (r && (r.bottom < 0 || r.top > window.innerHeight || r.right < 0 || r.left > window.innerWidth)) {\n
                    this.close();\n
                } else {\n
                    this.panelVisible = true;\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
\n
        closePanel() {\n
            if (this._boundReposition) {\n
                window.removeEventListener('scroll', this._boundReposition, true);\n
                window.removeEventListener('resize', this._boundReposition);\n
                this._boundReposition = null;\n
            }\n
        },\n
\n
        get panelStyle() {\n
            // No rect yet (e.g. during the open tick): park the panel off-screen\n
            // so it never flashes at the viewport origin.\n
            if (!this.triggerRect) {\n
                return 'position: fixed; top: -9999px; left: -9999px; z-index: 60; visibility: hidden;';\n
            }\n
\n
            const r = this.triggerRect;\n
            const gap = 8; // ~mt-2\n
            const style = {\n
                position: 'fixed',\n
                top: (r.bottom + gap) + 'px',\n
                zIndex: 60,\n
                visibility: this.panelVisible ? 'visible' : 'hidden',\n
            };\n
\n
            if (false) {\n
                style.left = r.left + 'px';\n
            } else {\n
                // align right (default): right-align panel to the trigger's right edge.\n
                style.right = (window.innerWidth - r.right) + 'px';\n
            }\n
\n
            return Object.entries(style).map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join('; ');\n
        },\n
\n
        init() {\n
            this.$watch('open', (val) => {\n
                if (val) {\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => {\n
                        this.captureTrigger();\n
                        this.openPanel();\n
                    });\n
                } else {\n
                    this.closePanel();\n
                }\n
            });\n
        },\n
    }"\n
    @click.outside="close()"\n
    @close.stop="close()"\n
    class="relative"\n
>\n
    <div class="inline-flex" data-dropdown-trigger @click="toggle()">\n
        <button class="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 rounded-lg text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest hover:bg-primary-50 hover:text-primary-600 transition-all border border-slate-200 dark:border-dark-border group">\r\n
                                                    Action\r\n
                                                    <svg class="w-2.5 h-2.5 group-hover:rotate-180 transition-transform" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2.5" d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7"></path></svg>\r\n
                                                </button>\n
    </div>\n
\n
    \n
    <template x-teleport="body">\n
        <div\n
            x-show="open"\n
            x-cloak\n
            x-transition:enter="transition ease-out duration-200"\n
            x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 scale-95"\n
            x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 scale-100"\n
            x-transition:leave="transition ease-in duration-75"\n
            x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 scale-100"\n
            x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 scale-95"\n
            :style="panelStyle"\n
            @click="open = false"\n
            class="fixed rounded-lg shadow-dropdown border border-border dark:border-dark-border bg-card dark:bg-dark-card w-40 origin-top-right"\n
        >\n
            <div class="rounded-lg py-1.5 py-1 bg-white">\n

                                                        <svg class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-primary-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2.2" d="M17 17h2a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v4a2 2 0 002 2h2m2 4h6a2 2 0 002-2v-4a2 2 0 012-2H5a2 2 0 012 2v4a2 2 0 002 2zm8-12V5a2 2 0 00-2-2H9a2 2 0 00-2 2v4h10z"></path></svg>\r\n

\r\n

                'file-text':          'M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8zM14 2v6h6M16 13H8M16 17H8M10 9H8',\r\n

            },\r\n
\r\n
            openModal() {\r\n
                this.open = true;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                this.$nextTick(() => {\r\n
                    const el = document.getElementById('global-command-palette-input');\r\n
                    if (el) el.focus();\r\n
                });\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            closeModal() {\r\n
                this.open = false;\r\n
                this.query = '';\r\n
                this.results = {};\r\n
                this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            get flatResults() {\r\n
                const flat = [];\r\n
                for (const key in this.results) {\r\n
                    if (this.results[key] && this.results[key].items) {\r\n
                        this.results[key].items.forEach(item => flat.push(item));\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
                return flat;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            async doSearch() {\r\n
                const q = this.query.trim();\r\n
                const requestId = ++this.searchRequestId;\r\n
                if (this.searchController) {\r\n
                    this.searchController.abort();\r\n
                }\r\n
                if (q.length < 2) {\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                    this.loading = false;\r\n
                    return;\r\n
                }\r\n
                this.loading = true;\r\n
                const controller = new AbortController();\r\n
                this.searchController = controller;\r\n
                try {\r\n
                    const res = await fetch('http://localhost:8000/global-search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q), {\r\n
                        signal: controller.signal,\r\n
                        headers: {\r\n
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name=csrf-token]').content,\r\n
                            'Accept': 'application/json',\r\n
                        },\r\n
                    });\r\n
                    const data = await res.json();\r\n
                    if (requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = data.categories || {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = data.total_count || 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                } catch (e) {\r\n
                    if (e.name === 'AbortError' || requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                } finally {\r\n
                    if (requestId === this.searchRequestId) {\r\n
                        this.loading = false;\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            navigate(dir) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (!flat.length) return;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(flat.length - 1, this.activeIdx + dir));\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            selectActive() {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx]) {\r\n
                    window.location.href = flat[this.activeIdx].url;\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            isActive(item) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                return this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx] && flat[this.activeIdx].url === item.url;\r\n
            },\r\n
        }"\r\n
        @open-global-search.window="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.cmd.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.ctrl.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.escape.window="closeModal()"\r\n
        x-show="open"\r\n
        x-cloak\r\n
        class="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto"\r\n

                    <div class="shrink-0 mr-3">\r\n
