<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
        Alpine.store('notify', {\n
            toasts: [],\n
            _idCounter: 0,\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show a success toast.\n
             * @param {string} message\n
             * @param {number} duration  Auto-dismiss ms. Default 4000. 0 = never.\n
             */\n
            showSuccess(message, duration = 4000) {\n
                this._add('success', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show an error toast.\n
             * @param {string|string[]} message  Single string or array of validation errors.\n
             * @param {number}          duration  Default 0 = stays until dismissed.\n
             */\n
            showError(message, duration = 0) {\n
                // Normalize: if Laravel validation errors object {field: [msgs]} convert to flat array\n
                if (message && typeof message === 'object' && !Array.isArray(message)) {\n
                    message = Object.values(message).flat();\n
                }\n
                this._add('error', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * @private\n
             */\n
            _add(type, messages, duration) {\n
                const id = ++this._idCounter;\n
                const toast = { id, type, messages, duration, visible: true, progress: 100 };\n
                this.toasts.push(toast);\n
\n
                if (duration > 0) {\n
                    // After a short delay, start the progress bar shrink\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        const t = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                        if (t) t.progress = 0;\n
                    }, 50);\n
\n
                    // Auto-dismiss\n
                    setTimeout(() => this.dismiss(id), duration);\n
                }\n
\n
                // Keep max 5 toasts visible at once — remove oldest\n
                if (this.toasts.length > 5) {\n
                    this.dismiss(this.toasts[0].id);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            dismiss(id) {\n
                const toast = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                if (toast) {\n
                    toast.visible = false;\n
                    // Remove from array after leave animation completes\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        this.toasts = this.toasts.filter(t => t.id !== id);\n
                    }, 300);\n
                }\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        queueMicrotask(() => {\n
                                            });\n
    });\n
\n
    /**\n
     * ── Global convenience aliases ─────────────────────────────────\n
     * Callable from ANY script tag, AJAX handler, or Alpine component:\n
     *\n
     *   showSuccess('Item saved successfully.');\n
     *   showError('The brand name has already been taken.');\n
     *   showError(['Name required.', 'Price must be numeric.']);\n
     */\n
    window.showSuccess = (msg, duration = 4000) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    window.showError = (msg, duration = 0) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
</script>\n\r
\n
    \r\n
    \r\n
    \r\n

     x-init="initShortcuts()"\n
     @open-shortcuts-help.window="openHelpModal()"\n
     class="relative z-50">\n
\n
    \n
    <div x-show="inSequence"\n
         x-transition:enter="transition ease-out duration-200"\n
         x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n
         x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"\n
         x-transition:leave="transition ease-in duration-150"\n
         x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"\n
         x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n
         class="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 bg-slate-900/95 dark:bg-slate-800/95 text-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700/80 backdrop-blur-md"\n
         x-cloak>\n
        <div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-400 animate-ping"></div>\n
        <div class="flex flex-col">\n
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">\n
                <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Shortcut Sequence:</span>\n
                <span class="text-xs font-black text-primary-400 tracking-wide" x-text="activeModuleName"></span>\n
                <span class="text-slate-400">&gt;</span>\n

            <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Press second key or <kbd class="font-mono bg-slate-800 px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] text-slate-300">Esc</kbd> to cancel</p>\n

    function keyboardShortcutsHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            inSequence: false,\n
            pendingModuleKey: null,\n
            activeModuleName: '',\n
            sequenceTimer: null,\n
            timeoutDuration: 1800, // ~1.8 seconds timeout\n
\n
            initShortcuts() {\n
                window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleKeyDown(e));\n
            },\n
\n
            isEditableTarget(target) {\n
                if (!target) return false;\n
                const tagName = target.tagName ? target.tagName.toUpperCase() : '';\n
                if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(tagName)) return true;\n
                if (target.isContentEditable) return true;\n
                if (target.closest && target.closest('[data-global-search-modal]')) {\n
                    return true;\n
                }\n
                return false;\n
            },\n
\n
            openHelpModal() {\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('toggle-shortcuts-modal', { detail: { open: true } }));\n
            },\n
\n
            handleKeyDown(e) {\n
                // Disabled on mobile and tablet screens (< 1024px)\n
                if (window.innerWidth < 1024) return;\n
\n
                // Never intercept if focused on input, textarea, select, or search box\n
                if (this.isEditableTarget(e.target)) return;\n
\n
                const key = e.key ? e.key.toUpperCase() : '';\n
\n
                // If in sequence mode\n
                if (this.inSequence) {\n
                    if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.cancelSequence();\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Ignore standalone modifier presses while waiting for the 2nd key\n
                    if (['CONTROL', 'ALT', 'SHIFT', 'META'].includes(key)) {\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.executeSecondKey(key);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Check for Help Modal shortcut: Ctrl+Alt+? or Shift+?\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && (e.key === '?' || e.key === '/')) {\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.openHelpModal();\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Trigger First Key: Ctrl + Alt + [Module Key]\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && !e.shiftKey && !e.metaKey) {\n
                    if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS && window.APP_SHORTCUTS[key]) {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.startSequence(key);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            startSequence(moduleKey) {\n
                this.inSequence = true;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = moduleKey;\n
                this.activeModuleName = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[moduleKey]?.module || moduleKey;\n
\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                this.sequenceTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n
                    this.cancelSequence();\n
                }, this.timeoutDuration);\n
            },\n
\n
            cancelSequence() {\n
                this.inSequence = false;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = null;\n
                this.activeModuleName = '';\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) {\n
                    clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                    this.sequenceTimer = null;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            executeSecondKey(pageKey) {\n
                const moduleData = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[this.pendingModuleKey];\n
                this.cancelSequence();\n
\n
                if (!moduleData || !moduleData.items) return;\n
\n
                const targetItem = moduleData.items.find(item => item.page_key === pageKey);\n
\n
                if (!targetItem) {\n
                    // Second key not found in this module\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Strict permission enforcement\n
                if (!targetItem.has_permission) {\n
                    if (typeof window.showError === 'function') {\n
                        window.showError("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    } else {\n
                        alert("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    }\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Navigate to target route\n
                if (targetItem.url && targetItem.url !== '#') {\n
                    window.location.href = targetItem.url;\n
                }\n
            }\n
        };\n
    }\n
</script>\n

<script>\n
    function shortcutsModalHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            isOpen: false,\n
            searchQuery: '',\n
            expandedModules: {},\n
            allExpanded: true,\n
\n
            init() {\n
                // By default expand all modules\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = true;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            openModal(open = true) {\n
                this.isOpen = open;\n
                if (open) {\n
                    this.init();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            closeModal() {\n
                this.isOpen = false;\n
                this.searchQuery = '';\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleModule(key) {\n
                this.expandedModules[key] = !this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            isModuleExpanded(key) {\n
                if (this.searchQuery.trim().length > 0) return true;\n
                return !!this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleAll() {\n
                this.allExpanded = !this.allExpanded;\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = this.allExpanded;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            get filteredModules() {\n
                if (!window.APP_SHORTCUTS) return {};\n
                const q = this.searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\n
                if (!q) return window.APP_SHORTCUTS;\n
\n
                const result = {};\n
                Object.entries(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(([modKey, mod]) => {\n
                    const modMatches = mod.module.toLowerCase().includes(q) || ('ctrl+alt+' + modKey.toLowerCase()).includes(q);\n
                    const matchingItems = mod.items.filter(item => {\n
                        return modMatches ||\n
                               item.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n
                               item.page_key.toLowerCase() === q ||\n
                               item.sequence.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n
                    });\n
\n
                    if (matchingItems.length > 0) {\n
                        result[modKey] = {\n
                            ...mod,\n
                            items: matchingItems\n
                        };\n
                    }\n
                });\n
                return result;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    }\n
</script>\n\r

    <div\r\n
        x-data="{\r\n
            open: false,\r\n
            query: '',\r\n
            results: {},\r\n
            totalCount: 0,\r\n
            loading: false,\r\n
            activeIdx: -1,\r\n

            searchController: null,\r\n
\r\n

                'package':            'M21 16V8a2 2 0 00-1-1.73l-7-4a2 2 0 00-2 0l-7 4A2 2 0 003 8v8a2 2 0 001 1.73l7 4a2 2 0 002 0l7-4A2 2 0 0021 16z',\r\n
                'wrench':             'M14.7 6.3a1 1 0 000 1.4l1.6 1.6a1 1 0 001.4 0l3.77-3.77a6 6 0 01-7.94 7.94l-6.91 6.91a2.12 2.12 0 01-3-3l6.91-6.91a6 6 0 017.94-7.94l-3.76 3.76z',\r\n
                'user':               'M20 21v-2a4 4 0 00-4-4H8a4 4 0 00-4 4v2M12 11a4 4 0 100-8 4 4 0 000 8z',\r\n
                'truck':              'M5 17H3a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h11a2 2 0 012 2v3m0 0h4l3 3v4h-7V8zM16 17a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0zM7 17a2 2 0 11-4 0 2 2 0 014 0z',\r\n
                'file-text':          'M14 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v16a2 2 0 002 2h12a2 2 0 002-2V8zM14 2v6h6M16 13H8M16 17H8M10 9H8',\r\n
                'shopping-cart':      'M6 2L3 6v14a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V6l-3-4zM3 6h18M16 10a4 4 0 01-8 0',\r\n

                'rotate-ccw':         'M1 4v6h6M3.51 15a9 9 0 101.85-4.36L1 10',\r\n

            },\r\n
\r\n
            openModal() {\r\n
                this.open = true;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                this.$nextTick(() => {\r\n
                    const el = document.getElementById('global-command-palette-input');\r\n
                    if (el) el.focus();\r\n
                });\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            closeModal() {\r\n
                this.open = false;\r\n
                this.query = '';\r\n
                this.results = {};\r\n
                this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            get flatResults() {\r\n
                const flat = [];\r\n
                for (const key in this.results) {\r\n
                    if (this.results[key] && this.results[key].items) {\r\n
                        this.results[key].items.forEach(item => flat.push(item));\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
                return flat;\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            async doSearch() {\r\n
                const q = this.query.trim();\r\n
                const requestId = ++this.searchRequestId;\r\n
                if (this.searchController) {\r\n
                    this.searchController.abort();\r\n
                }\r\n
                if (q.length < 2) {\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                    this.loading = false;\r\n
                    return;\r\n
                }\r\n
                this.loading = true;\r\n
                const controller = new AbortController();\r\n
                this.searchController = controller;\r\n
                try {\r\n
                    const res = await fetch('http://localhost:8000/global-search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q), {\r\n
                        signal: controller.signal,\r\n
                        headers: {\r\n
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name=csrf-token]').content,\r\n
                            'Accept': 'application/json',\r\n
                        },\r\n
                    });\r\n
                    const data = await res.json();\r\n
                    if (requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = data.categories || {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = data.total_count || 0;\r\n
                    this.activeIdx = -1;\r\n
                } catch (e) {\r\n
                    if (e.name === 'AbortError' || requestId !== this.searchRequestId) return;\r\n
                    this.results = {};\r\n
                    this.totalCount = 0;\r\n
                } finally {\r\n
                    if (requestId === this.searchRequestId) {\r\n
                        this.loading = false;\r\n
                    }\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            navigate(dir) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (!flat.length) return;\r\n
                this.activeIdx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(flat.length - 1, this.activeIdx + dir));\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            selectActive() {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                if (this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx]) {\r\n
                    window.location.href = flat[this.activeIdx].url;\r\n
                }\r\n
            },\r\n
\r\n
            isActive(item) {\r\n
                const flat = this.flatResults;\r\n
                return this.activeIdx >= 0 && flat[this.activeIdx] && flat[this.activeIdx].url === item.url;\r\n
            },\r\n
        }"\r\n
        @open-global-search.window="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.cmd.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.window.ctrl.k.prevent="openModal()"\r\n
        @keydown.escape.window="closeModal()"\r\n
        x-show="open"\r\n
        x-cloak\r\n
        class="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto"\r\n
        role="dialog"\r\n
        aria-modal="true">\r\n
\r\n

            x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"\r\n

            @click="closeModal()"\r\n
            class="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm transition-opacity"></div>\r\n
\r\n

\r\n

                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z"/>\r\n
                        </svg>\r\n
                        <svg x-show="loading" x-cloak class="w-5 h-5 text-primary-500 animate-spin" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">\r\n
                            <circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"/>\r\n
                            <path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4z"/>\r\n
                        </svg>\r\n

                    <input\r\n

    <template x-for="toast in $store.notify.toasts" :key="toast.id">\n

            class="pointer-events-auto relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border"\n
            :class="{\n
                'bg-white dark:bg-dark-card border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-500/30 shadow-emerald-100 dark:shadow-none': toast.type === 'success',\n

                }"\n

            >\n

<script>\n
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
        Alpine.store('notify', {\n
            toasts: [],\n
            _idCounter: 0,\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show a success toast.\n
             * @param {string} message\n
             * @param {number} duration  Auto-dismiss ms. Default 4000. 0 = never.\n
             */\n
            showSuccess(message, duration = 4000) {\n
                this._add('success', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * Show an error toast.\n
             * @param {string|string[]} message  Single string or array of validation errors.\n
             * @param {number}          duration  Default 0 = stays until dismissed.\n
             */\n
            showError(message, duration = 0) {\n
                // Normalize: if Laravel validation errors object {field: [msgs]} convert to flat array\n
                if (message && typeof message === 'object' && !Array.isArray(message)) {\n
                    message = Object.values(message).flat();\n
                }\n
                this._add('error', message, duration);\n
            },\n
\n
            /**\n
             * @private\n
             */\n
            _add(type, messages, duration) {\n
                const id = ++this._idCounter;\n
                const toast = { id, type, messages, duration, visible: true, progress: 100 };\n
                this.toasts.push(toast);\n
\n
                if (duration > 0) {\n
                    // After a short delay, start the progress bar shrink\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        const t = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                        if (t) t.progress = 0;\n
                    }, 50);\n
\n
                    // Auto-dismiss\n
                    setTimeout(() => this.dismiss(id), duration);\n
                }\n
\n
                // Keep max 5 toasts visible at once — remove oldest\n
                if (this.toasts.length > 5) {\n
                    this.dismiss(this.toasts[0].id);\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            dismiss(id) {\n
                const toast = this.toasts.find(t => t.id === id);\n
                if (toast) {\n
                    toast.visible = false;\n
                    // Remove from array after leave animation completes\n
                    setTimeout(() => {\n
                        this.toasts = this.toasts.filter(t => t.id !== id);\n
                    }, 300);\n
                }\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        queueMicrotask(() => {\n
                                            });\n
    });\n
\n
    /**\n
     * ── Global convenience aliases ─────────────────────────────────\n
     * Callable from ANY script tag, AJAX handler, or Alpine component:\n
     *\n
     *   showSuccess('Item saved successfully.');\n
     *   showError('The brand name has already been taken.');\n
     *   showError(['Name required.', 'Price must be numeric.']);\n
     */\n
    window.showSuccess = (msg, duration = 4000) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showSuccess(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    window.showError = (msg, duration = 0) => {\n
        if (typeof Alpine !== 'undefined' && Alpine.store) {\n
            Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
        } else {\n
            document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {\n
                Alpine.store('notify').showError(msg, duration);\n
            }, { once: true });\n
        }\n
    };\n
</script>\n\r
\n
    \r\n
    \r\n
    \r\n
    <div x-data="keyboardShortcutsHandler()" \n
     x-init="initShortcuts()"\n
     @open-shortcuts-help.window="openHelpModal()"\n
     class="relative z-50">\n

    <div x-show="inSequence"\n

         x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n

         x-transition:leave="transition ease-in duration-150"\n
         x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 scale-100"\n
         x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 translate-y-4 scale-95"\n
         class="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 bg-slate-900/95 dark:bg-slate-800/95 text-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700/80 backdrop-blur-md"\n
         x-cloak>\n
        <div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-400 animate-ping"></div>\n

                <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300">Shortcut Sequence:</span>\n
                <span class="text-xs font-black text-primary-400 tracking-wide" x-text="activeModuleName"></span>\n
                <span class="text-slate-400">&gt;</span>\n
                <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-mono font-bold bg-primary-500/20 text-primary-300 rounded border border-primary-400/30 animate-pulse">_</span>\n
            </div>\n
            <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Press second key or <kbd class="font-mono bg-slate-800 px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] text-slate-300">Esc</kbd> to cancel</p>\n
        </div>\n

    function keyboardShortcutsHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            inSequence: false,\n
            pendingModuleKey: null,\n
            activeModuleName: '',\n
            sequenceTimer: null,\n
            timeoutDuration: 1800, // ~1.8 seconds timeout\n
\n
            initShortcuts() {\n
                window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => this.handleKeyDown(e));\n
            },\n
\n
            isEditableTarget(target) {\n
                if (!target) return false;\n
                const tagName = target.tagName ? target.tagName.toUpperCase() : '';\n
                if (['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(tagName)) return true;\n
                if (target.isContentEditable) return true;\n
                if (target.closest && target.closest('[data-global-search-modal]')) {\n
                    return true;\n
                }\n
                return false;\n
            },\n
\n
            openHelpModal() {\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('toggle-shortcuts-modal', { detail: { open: true } }));\n
            },\n
\n
            handleKeyDown(e) {\n
                // Disabled on mobile and tablet screens (< 1024px)\n
                if (window.innerWidth < 1024) return;\n
\n
                // Never intercept if focused on input, textarea, select, or search box\n
                if (this.isEditableTarget(e.target)) return;\n
\n
                const key = e.key ? e.key.toUpperCase() : '';\n
\n
                // If in sequence mode\n
                if (this.inSequence) {\n
                    if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.cancelSequence();\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Ignore standalone modifier presses while waiting for the 2nd key\n
                    if (['CONTROL', 'ALT', 'SHIFT', 'META'].includes(key)) {\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.executeSecondKey(key);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Check for Help Modal shortcut: Ctrl+Alt+? or Shift+?\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && (e.key === '?' || e.key === '/')) {\n
                    e.preventDefault();\n
                    this.openHelpModal();\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Trigger First Key: Ctrl + Alt + [Module Key]\n
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.altKey && !e.shiftKey && !e.metaKey) {\n
                    if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS && window.APP_SHORTCUTS[key]) {\n
                        e.preventDefault();\n
                        this.startSequence(key);\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            startSequence(moduleKey) {\n
                this.inSequence = true;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = moduleKey;\n
                this.activeModuleName = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[moduleKey]?.module || moduleKey;\n
\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                this.sequenceTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n
                    this.cancelSequence();\n
                }, this.timeoutDuration);\n
            },\n
\n
            cancelSequence() {\n
                this.inSequence = false;\n
                this.pendingModuleKey = null;\n
                this.activeModuleName = '';\n
                if (this.sequenceTimer) {\n
                    clearTimeout(this.sequenceTimer);\n
                    this.sequenceTimer = null;\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            executeSecondKey(pageKey) {\n
                const moduleData = window.APP_SHORTCUTS[this.pendingModuleKey];\n
                this.cancelSequence();\n
\n
                if (!moduleData || !moduleData.items) return;\n
\n
                const targetItem = moduleData.items.find(item => item.page_key === pageKey);\n
\n
                if (!targetItem) {\n
                    // Second key not found in this module\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Strict permission enforcement\n
                if (!targetItem.has_permission) {\n
                    if (typeof window.showError === 'function') {\n
                        window.showError("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    } else {\n
                        alert("You don't have permission to access this page");\n
                    }\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                // Navigate to target route\n
                if (targetItem.url && targetItem.url !== '#') {\n
                    window.location.href = targetItem.url;\n
                }\n
            }\n

<script>\n
    function shortcutsModalHandler() {\n
        return {\n
            isOpen: false,\n
            searchQuery: '',\n
            expandedModules: {},\n
            allExpanded: true,\n
\n
            init() {\n
                // By default expand all modules\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = true;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            openModal(open = true) {\n
                this.isOpen = open;\n
                if (open) {\n
                    this.init();\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            closeModal() {\n
                this.isOpen = false;\n
                this.searchQuery = '';\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleModule(key) {\n
                this.expandedModules[key] = !this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            isModuleExpanded(key) {\n
                if (this.searchQuery.trim().length > 0) return true;\n
                return !!this.expandedModules[key];\n
            },\n
\n
            toggleAll() {\n
                this.allExpanded = !this.allExpanded;\n
                if (window.APP_SHORTCUTS) {\n
                    Object.keys(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(k => {\n
                        this.expandedModules[k] = this.allExpanded;\n
                    });\n
                }\n
            },\n
\n
            get filteredModules() {\n
                if (!window.APP_SHORTCUTS) return {};\n
                const q = this.searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();\n
                if (!q) return window.APP_SHORTCUTS;\n
\n
                const result = {};\n
                Object.entries(window.APP_SHORTCUTS).forEach(([modKey, mod]) => {\n
                    const modMatches = mod.module.toLowerCase().includes(q) || ('ctrl+alt+' + modKey.toLowerCase()).includes(q);\n
                    const matchingItems = mod.items.filter(item => {\n
                        return modMatches ||\n
                               item.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n
                               item.page_key.toLowerCase() === q ||\n
                               item.sequence.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n
                    });\n
\n
                    if (matchingItems.length > 0) {\n
                        result[modKey] = {\n
                            ...mod,\n
                            items: matchingItems\n
                        };\n
                    }\n
                });\n
                return result;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    }\n
</script>\n\r
\n
        <script>\r\n
        function salesReturnForm() {\r\n
            return {\r\n
                isSubmitting: false,\r\n
                serialModalActive: false,\r\n
                activeItemIndex: null,\r\n
                saleDue: 0,\r\n
                salePaid: 200,\r\n
                saleGrandTotal: 100,\r\n
                form: {\r\n
                    sales_id: '1',\r\n
                    return_date: '2026-09-19',\r\n
                    reference_no: '',\r\n
                    note: '',\r\n
                    subtotal: 0,\r\n
                    grand_total: 0,\r\n
                    paid_amount: 0,\r\n
                    account_id: '',\r\n
                    payment_type: 'Cash',\r\n
                    items: [{"item_id":1,"item_name":"Credit Item","sku":"ITM-CREDIT-01","sold_qty":2,"already_returned":1,"remaining_qty":1,"return_qty":1,"price_per_unit":500,"tax_amt":0,"discount_amt":0,"total_cost":0,"is_serialized":false,"available_serials":[],"serials":[]}]\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                init() {\r\n
                    // Set initial refund to maximum allowable cash refund\r\n
                    this.$nextTick(() => {\r\n
                        this.form.paid_amount = this.calculateMaxCashRefund;\r\n
                    });\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                updateQty(index, delta) {\r\n
                    let item = this.form.items[index];\r\n
                    let newQty = Number(item.return_qty) + delta;\r\n
                    if (newQty >= 0 && newQty <= item.remaining_qty) {\r\n
                        item.return_qty = newQty;\r\n
                        if (item.is_serialized && item.serials.length > newQty) {\r\n
                            item.serials = item.serials.slice(0, newQty);\r\n
                        }\r\n
                        this.autoAdjustRefund();\r\n
                    }\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                validateQty(index) {\r\n
                    let item = this.form.items[index];\r\n
                    if (item.return_qty < 0) item.return_qty = 0;\r\n
                    if (item.return_qty > item.remaining_qty) item.return_qty = item.remaining_qty;\r\n
                    \r\n
                    if (item.is_serialized && item.serials.length > item.return_qty) {\r\n
                        item.serials = item.serials.slice(0, Math.floor(item.return_qty));\r\n
                    }\r\n
                    this.autoAdjustRefund();\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                autoAdjustRefund() {\r\n
                    if (this.form.paid_amount > this.calculateMaxCashRefund) {\r\n
                        this.form.paid_amount = this.calculateMaxCashRefund;\r\n
                    }\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                calculateItemTotal(item) {\r\n
                    if (item.return_qty <= 0) return 0;\r\n
                    let total = (item.price_per_unit - item.discount_amt + item.tax_amt) * item.return_qty;\r\n
                    item.total_cost = total;\r\n
                    return total;\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get calculateSubtotal() {\r\n
                    let sub = this.form.items.reduce((acc, item) => {\r\n
                        return acc + ((item.price_per_unit - item.discount_amt) * item.return_qty);\r\n
                    }, 0);\r\n
                    this.form.subtotal = sub;\r\n
                    return sub;\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get calculateTotalTax() {\r\n
                    return this.form.items.reduce((acc, item) => {\r\n
                        return acc + (item.tax_amt * item.return_qty);\r\n
                    }, 0);\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get calculateTotalDiscount() {\r\n
                    return this.form.items.reduce((acc, item) => {\r\n
                        return acc + (item.discount_amt * item.return_qty);\r\n
                    }, 0);\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get calculateGrandTotal() {\r\n
                    let total = Math.round((this.calculateSubtotal + this.calculateTotalTax) * 100) / 100;\r\n
                    this.form.grand_total = total;\r\n
                    return total;\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get calculateDueOffset() {\r\n
                    return Math.min(this.calculateGrandTotal, this.saleDue);\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get calculateMaxCashRefund() {\r\n
                    return Math.max(0, Math.round((this.calculateGrandTotal - this.calculateDueOffset) * 100) / 100);\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get newSaleDue() {\r\n
                    return Math.max(0, Math.round((this.saleDue - this.calculateDueOffset) * 100) / 100);\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                get isOverRefunding() {\r\n
                    return (parseFloat(this.form.paid_amount) || 0) > (this.calculateMaxCashRefund + 0.001);\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                openSerialModal(index) {\r\n
                    this.activeItemIndex = index;\r\n
                    this.serialModalActive = true;\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                toggleSerial(sn) {\r\n
                    let item = this.form.items[this.activeItemIndex];\r\n
                    let idx = item.serials.indexOf(sn);\r\n
                    if (idx > -1) {\r\n
                        item.serials.splice(idx, 1);\r\n
                    } else {\r\n
                        if (item.serials.length < item.return_qty) {\r\n
                            item.serials.push(sn);\r\n
                        } else {\r\n
                            showError('Serial numbers count cannot exceed return quantity');\r\n
                        }\r\n
                    }\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                isSerialSelected(sn) {\r\n
                    if (this.activeItemIndex === null) return false;\r\n
                    return this.form.items[this.activeItemIndex].serials.includes(sn);\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                formatCurrency(val) {\r\n
                    return '$' + Number(val).toLocaleString(undefined, {minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2});\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                formatNumber(val) {\r\n
                    return Number(val).toLocaleString(undefined, {minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2});\r\n
                },\r\n
\r\n
                submitForm() {\r\n
                    if (this.calculateGrandTotal <= 0) {\r\n
                        showError('Return quantity must be greater than 0 for at least one item');\r\n
                        return;\r\n
                    }\r\n
\r\n
                    if (this.isOverRefunding) {\r\n
                        showError(`Refund amount cannot exceed the maximum allowable cash refund of ${this.formatCurrency(this.calculateMaxCashRefund)}`);\r\n
                        return;\r\n
                    }\r\n
\r\n
                    // Validate Serial Numbers selection\r\n
                    for (let item of this.form.items) {\r\n
                        if (item.is_serialized && item.return_qty > 0 && item.serials.length !== Math.floor(item.return_qty)) {\r\n
                            showError(`Please select ${Math.floor(item.return_qty)} serial numbers for item: ${item.item_name}`);\r\n
                            return;\r\n
                        }\r\n
                    }\r\n
\r\n
                    if (this.form.paid_amount > 0 && !this.form.account_id) {\r\n
                        showError('Please select an account for the refund payment');\r\n
                        return;\r\n
                    }\r\n
\r\n
                    this.isSubmitting = true;\r\n
\r\n
                    fetch('http://localhost:8000/sales/return/store', {\r\n
                        method: 'POST',\r\n
                        headers: {\r\n
                            'Content-Type': 'application/json',\r\n
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': 'zhetjovSAI6HwRio7NVHEPxiFRNiPkGTZBhZXQgY'\r\n
                        },\r\n
                        body: JSON.stringify(this.form)\r\n
                    })\r\n
                    .then(response => response.json())\r\n
                    .then(data => {\r\n
                        if (data.success) {\r\n
                            showSuccess(data.message || 'Sales Return created successfully!');\r\n
                            setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = data.redirect || "http://localhost:8000/sales/returns"; }, 800);\r\n
                        } else {\r\n
                            showError(data.message || 'Failed to create sales return.');\r\n
                            this.isSubmitting = false;\r\n
                        }\r\n
                    })\r\n
                    .catch(error => {\r\n
                        console.error('Error:', error);\r\n
                        showError('Something went wrong. Please check console for details.');\r\n
                        this.isSubmitting = false;\r\n
                    });\r\n
                }\r\n
            }\r\n
        }\r\n
    </script>\r\n
    \r\n
\r\n
</body>\r\n
\r\n
</html>\r\n
